PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima\n\n/-!\n# Q01 -- Krein positive-sector witness vs. no-go\n\nThis module makes precise, and kernel-checks, the central separation of the Q01\nanswer (`Q01_answer.md`, Theorem A + Finding A): the finite Kugo-Ojima quotient\nform on `ker Q / range Q` is nondegenerate under the algebraic hypotheses of\n`finite_kugo_ojima`, but physical positivity of that quotient is a separate\nquestion governed by the inertia of the ambient Krein form.\n\nWe witness both sides of the separation with two models on\n`W := EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin 3)` that share the same nilpotent\nKrein-self-adjoint charge `Q` and differ only in the sign of one diagonal entry\nof the Krein Gram matrix `J`:\n\n* `Jpos` has Gram `[[0,1,0],[1,0,0],[0,0,1]]`: inertia `(2,1)`, with the\n  constraint direction `e0` isotropic.  The induced form on `ker Q / range Q`\n  is one-dimensional and positive definite.\n* `Jneg` has Gram `[[0,1,0],[1,0,0],[0,0,-1]]`: inertia `(1,2)`.  With the\n  identical `Q`, `finite_kugo_ojima` still certifies quotient nondegeneracy, but\n  the surviving physical class has negative Krein norm.\n\nScope / honesty: this is PROVED finite linear algebra.  The physical reading\nthat `dim(physical sector) = ind(D)` and that `p > q` is the nonvacuity\ncondition is the MEMO/OPEN interpretation layer of `Q01_answer.md`, of which\nthese are two extremal finite witnesses.\n\nProvenance: Aristotle project\n`ne-q01-krein-positive-sector-witness-or-no-go-audit-20260707`; clean-room\nfinite linear algebra built on `KugoOjima.finite_kugo_ojima`.\n-/\n\nopen scoped InnerProductSpace\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima\n\n/-- The shared witness space `ℂ³` with its Euclidean Hilbert inner product. -/\nabbrev W : Type := EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin 3)\n\n/-- The shared nilpotent Krein-self-adjoint charge matrix\n`Q = [[0,1,0],[0,0,0],[0,0,0]]` (`Q e₁ = e₀`, `Q e₀ = Q e₂ = 0`). -/\nnoncomputable def Qmat : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ℂ := !![0, 1, 0; 0, 0, 0; 0, 0, 0]\n\n/-- The shared BRST-type charge operator. -/\nnoncomputable def Qop : W →ₗ[ℂ] W := Matrix.toEuclideanLin Qmat\n\n/-- Krein Gram of the positive-sector model: inertia `(2,1)`, `e₀` isotropic. -/\nnoncomputable def GmatPos : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ℂ := !![0, 1, 0; 1, 0, 0; 0, 0, 1]\n\n/-- The fundamental symmetry of the positive-sector model. -/\nnoncomputable def Jpos : W →ₗ[ℂ] W := Matrix.toEuclideanLin GmatPos\n\n/-- Krein Gram of the no-go model: inertia `(1,2)`, identical to `GmatPos`\nexcept the sign of the single non-null direction `e₂`. -/\nnoncomputable def GmatNeg : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ℂ := !![0, 1, 0; 1, 0, 0; 0, 0, -1]\n\n/-- The fundamental symmetry of the no-go model. -/\nnoncomputable def Jneg : W →ₗ[ℂ] W := Matrix.toEuclideanLin GmatNeg\n\n/-- The surviving physical class representative. -/\nnoncomputable def e2 : W := EuclideanSpace.single 2 1\n\n/-! ### Shared algebraic facts -/\n\ntheorem Qop_sq : Qop ∘ₗ Qop = 0 := by\n  ext x\n  rename_i i\n  fin_cases i <;> simp +decide [Qop, Qmat, Matrix.mulVec]\n\ntheorem Jpos_involutive : Jpos ∘ₗ Jpos = LinearMap.id := by\n  ext x\n  rename_i i\n  fin_cases i <;> simp +decide [Jpos, GmatPos] <;> rfl\n\ntheorem Jneg_involutive : Jneg ∘ₗ Jneg = LinearMap.id := by\n  ext x\n  simp [Jneg]\n  rename_i i\n  fin_cases i <;>\n    norm_num [Fin.sum_univ_succ, Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, GmatNeg] <;>\n    rfl\n\ntheorem Qop_kreinAdjoint_pos : kreinAdjoint Jpos Qop = Qop := by\n  ext x\n  unfold Qop\n  simp +decide [Matrix.toEuclideanLin]\n  unfold kreinAdjoint\n  erw [show (Matrix.toLpLin 2 2 Qmat).adjoint =\n    Matrix.toLpLin 2 2 (Qmat.conjTranspose) from ?_]\n  · simp +decide [Jpos, GmatPos, Qmat]\n    rename_i i\n    fin_cases i <;> simp +decide [Matrix.vecHead, Matrix.vecTail, Matrix.vecMul]\n  · ext\n    simp +decide [Matrix.toEuclideanLin_conjTranspose_eq_adjoint]\n\ntheorem Qop_kreinAdjoint_neg : kreinAdjoint Jneg Qop = Qop := by\n  refine' LinearMap.ext fun x => _\n  convert Matrix.toLpLin_apply 2 2 (GmatNeg * Qmat.conjTranspose * GmatNeg) x using 1\n  · have hJneg : Jneg = Matrix.toEuclideanLin GmatNeg := by\n      rfl\n    have hQop : Qop = Matrix.toEuclideanLin Qmat := by\n      rfl\n    simp [hJneg, hQop, Matrix.toEuclideanLin_conjTranspose_eq_adjoint]\n    rfl\n  · refine' congr_arg (fun m => Matrix.toEuclideanLin m x) _\n    ext i j\n    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> norm_num [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_succ] <;>\n      simp +decide [Qmat, GmatNeg]\n\ntheorem e2_mem_ker : e2 ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop := by\n  ext i\n  fin_cases i <;> simp +decide [Qop, Qmat, e2]\n\ntheorem e2_not_mem_range : e2 ∉ LinearMap.range Qop := by\n  unfold Qop e2\n  norm_num [Qmat, Matrix.toEuclideanLin]\n  intro x hx\n  have := congr_arg (fun y => y.ofLp 2) hx\n  norm_num [Matrix.mulVec] at this\n  simp_all +decide [dotProduct, Fin.sum_univ_three]\n\ntheorem kreinForm_pos_e2 : kreinForm Jpos e2 e2 = 1 := by\n  have hJpos_e2 : Jpos e2 = e2 := by\n    simp [Jpos, GmatPos]\n    ext i\n    fin_cases i <;> simp +decide [Matrix.toLpLin_apply, e2]\n  simp [kreinForm, hJpos_e2]\n  exact Or.inl (by unfold e2; norm_num)\n\ntheorem kreinForm_neg_e2 : kreinForm Jneg e2 e2 = -1 := by\n  convert congr_arg (fun x : W => inner ℂ e2 x) (show Jneg e2 = -e2 from _) using 1\n  · norm_num [e2]\n  · ext i\n    fin_cases i <;> norm_num [Jneg, GmatNeg, e2] <;> simp +decide\n\n/-- On the positive-sector model, the Krein form is positive semidefinite on the\nwhole representative space `ker Q`. -/\ntheorem kreinForm_pos_nonneg_on_ker (x : W) (hx : x ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop) :\n    0 ≤ (kreinForm Jpos x x).re := by\n  unfold Qop at hx\n  unfold Qmat at hx\n  simp_all +decide [Matrix.toEuclideanLin]\n  simp_all +decide [funext_iff, Fin.forall_fin_succ, Matrix.toLpLin]\n  simp_all +decide [Matrix.vecHead, Matrix.vecTail, kreinForm, Jpos, GmatPos]\n  simp_all +decide [Matrix.toEuclideanLin, Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, Fin.sum_univ_three, inner]\n  nlinarith\n\n/-- On the positive-sector model, the induced form is definite: a representative\nwith zero Krein norm lies in `range Q`, i.e. is null in the quotient. -/\ntheorem kreinForm_pos_definite_on_quotient (x : W) (hx : x ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop)\n    (h0 : (kreinForm Jpos x x).re = 0) : x ∈ LinearMap.range Qop := by\n  have h_expansion : (kreinForm Jpos x x).re = x.ofLp 2 * star x.ofLp 2 := by\n    unfold Qop at *\n    simp_all +decide [Matrix.toEuclideanLin]\n    simp_all +decide [Qmat, Matrix.toLpLin]\n    unfold kreinForm Jpos at h0\n    simp_all +decide\n    unfold GmatPos at h0\n    simp_all +decide [Matrix.toEuclideanLin, Matrix.vecHead, Matrix.vecTail]\n    simp_all +decide [Matrix.toLpLin, dotProduct, Fin.sum_univ_three, inner]\n    exact Complex.ext (by norm_num; nlinarith) (by norm_num; nlinarith)\n  have hx_form : x = x.ofLp 0 • EuclideanSpace.single 0 1 := by\n    ext i\n    fin_cases i <;> simp_all +decide [Qop]\n    replace hx := congr_arg (fun y => y.ofLp 0) hx\n    simp_all +decide [Qmat]\n    exact hx\n  refine' ⟨EuclideanSpace.single 1 (x.ofLp 0), _⟩\n  rw [hx_form]\n  ext i\n  fin_cases i <;> norm_num [Qop, Qmat]\n\n/-! ### The nonvacuous positive-sector witness -/\n\n/-- Nonvacuous positive-sector witness, with inertia `(2,1)`.  `Q` is a nilpotent\nKrein-self-adjoint charge for `Jpos`, so `finite_kugo_ojima` applies.  The\nKugo-Ojima quotient `ker Q / range Q` is nonvacuous, the class of `e₂` is\nnonzero, and the induced form is positive definite on representatives modulo\n`range Q`. -/\ntheorem nonvacuous_positive_sector :\n    (Jpos ∘ₗ Jpos = LinearMap.id) ∧ (Qop ∘ₗ Qop = 0) ∧ (kreinAdjoint Jpos Qop = Qop) ∧\n    (∀ x ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop, x ∉ LinearMap.range Qop →\n      ∃ y ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop, kreinForm Jpos y x ≠ 0) ∧\n    (e2 ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop ∧ e2 ∉ LinearMap.range Qop) ∧\n    (∀ x ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop, 0 ≤ (kreinForm Jpos x x).re) ∧\n    (∀ x ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop, (kreinForm Jpos x x).re = 0 → x ∈ LinearMap.range Qop) ∧\n    0 < (kreinForm Jpos e2 e2).re := by\n  refine ⟨Jpos_involutive, Qop_sq, Qop_kreinAdjoint_pos, ?_, ⟨e2_mem_ker, e2_not_mem_range⟩,\n    kreinForm_pos_nonneg_on_ker, kreinForm_pos_definite_on_quotient, ?_⟩\n  · exact (finite_kugo_ojima Jpos Jpos_involutive Qop Qop_sq Qop_kreinAdjoint_pos).2.2\n  · rw [kreinForm_pos_e2]\n    norm_num\n\n/-! ### The sharp no-go -/\n\n/-- Sharp no-go, with inertia `(1,2)`.  With the identical nilpotent\nKrein-self-adjoint charge `Q`, the finite Kugo-Ojima theorem still certifies that\nthe quotient form on `ker Q / range Q` is nondegenerate, yet the surviving class\n`e₂` has strictly negative Krein norm.  The only change from\n`nonvacuous_positive_sector` is the sign of one ambient Gram entry. -/\ntheorem nondegenerate_but_indefinite_no_go :\n    (Jneg ∘ₗ Jneg = LinearMap.id) ∧ (Qop ∘ₗ Qop = 0) ∧ (kreinAdjoint Jneg Qop = Qop) ∧\n    (∀ x ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop, x ∉ LinearMap.range Qop →\n      ∃ y ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop, kreinForm Jneg y x ≠ 0) ∧\n    (e2 ∈ LinearMap.ker Qop ∧ e2 ∉ LinearMap.range Qop ∧\n      (kreinForm Jneg e2 e2).re < 0) := by\n  refine ⟨Jneg_involutive, Qop_sq, Qop_kreinAdjoint_neg, ?_,\n    ⟨e2_mem_ker, e2_not_mem_range, ?_⟩⟩\n  · exact (finite_kugo_ojima Jneg Jneg_involutive Qop Qop_sq Qop_kreinAdjoint_neg).2.2\n  · rw [kreinForm_neg_e2]\n    norm_num\n\n/-- info: 'PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima.nonvacuous_positive_sector' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] -/\n#guard_msgs (whitespace := lax) in\n#print axioms nonvacuous_positive_sector\n\n/-- info: 'PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima.nondegenerate_but_indefinite_no_go' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] -/\n#guard_msgs (whitespace := lax) in\n#print axioms nondegenerate_but_indefinite_no_go\n\nend PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima\n","numLines":223,"startLine":1,"totalLines":223}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TsohX6R4eg2C6HuiZrchFZ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\t# The signed mass-budget theorem, with a witness (K4 / roadmap S6)\n3\t\n4\tDRAFT (kernel-clean; no `s o r r y`). The overnight all-mass run's K4 -\n5\tthe flagship S6 deliverable, in its Amendment-A3 SIGNED form: the finite\n6\tmass budget of a state splits EXACTLY into aperture / closure / turn\n7\tshares that sum to one, with the shares SIGNED (not asserted positive\n8\tuntil the physical-sector positivity crux closes).\n9\t\n10\t## The two parts\n11\t\n12\t* `signed_budget_sum_one` (abstract): given the guard-pinned Weitzenboeck\n13\t  identity `4 . D² = Q_A + Q_C + 4 . Q_T` and ANY linear functional `ev`\n14\t  (the expectation `<psi, . psi>`) with `ev (D²) != 0`, the shares\n15\t  `b_A = ev Q_A / M²`, `b_C = ev Q_C / M²`, `b_T = 4 ev Q_T / M²`\n16\t  (`M² = 4 ev(D²)`) satisfy `b_A + b_C + b_T = 1`.\n17\t* `witness_budget_*` (vacuity guard): a concrete `2x2` single-edge\n18\t  carrier - `gamma = sigma_x`, `Gamma = sigma_z`, `nabla = 1`, `phi = 1`,\n19\t  `g = 2` - discharges every hypothesis of `carrier_square_assembly`, so\n20\t  the identity holds by construction; with `ev = trace` the closure share\n21\t  is exactly `0` (one edge -> no closure) and the budget is\n22\t  `b_A = 1/2, b_C = 0, b_T = 1/2` - a non-vacuous witness.\n23\t\n24\tThis is the 3-slot `D²` budget (matching the pinned assembly); the 4-slot\n25\t`D^#D` budget with the soldering-gradient share `E` awaits the `D^#D`\n26\tassembly. The physical reading\n33\t(non-turn dominance, hadron budget) is prose. No continuum,\n36\tno hadron-mass claim.\n37\t\n38\t## Provenance\n39\t\n40\tS6 shape from the QCD roadmap (Amendment A3, SevenChallenges finding 7) -\n41\t[comp]; built on the guard-pinned `carrier_square_assembly`\n42\t(`CarrierSquareAssembly.lean`) - [orig].\n43\t-/\n44\t\n45\timport PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.CarrierSquareAssembly\n46\t\n47\tnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.MassBudget\n48\t\n49\topen Matrix\n50\t\n51\t/-! ## The abstract signed budget -/\n52\t\n53\t/-- **Signed mass-budget decomposition of unity.** From the Weitzenboeck\n54\tidentity `4 . D² = Q_A + Q_C + 4 . Q_T` and a linear expectation `ev` with\n55\t`ev (D * D) != 0`, the aperture/closure/turn shares of `M² = 4 ev(D²)` sum\n56\tto one. Shares are signed - no positivity assumed. -/\n57\ttheorem signed_budget_sum_one {B : Type*} [Ring B] [Algebra ℂ B]\n58\t    (ev : B →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ) (D QA QC QT : B)\n59\t    (hid : (4 : ℂ) • (D * D) = QA + QC + (4 : ℂ) • QT)\n60\t    (hM : ev (D * D) ≠ 0) :\n61\t    ev QA / (4 * ev (D * D)) + ev QC / (4 * ev (D * D))\n62\t        + (4 * ev QT) / (4 * ev (D * D)) = 1 := by\n63\t  have hev : (4 : ℂ) * ev (D * D) = ev QA + ev QC + 4 * ev QT := by\n64\t    have h := congrArg ev hid\n65\t    rw [map_smul, map_add, map_add, map_smul, smul_eq_mul, smul_eq_mul] at h\n66\t    exact h\n67\t  have hs : (4 : ℂ) * ev (D * D) ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero (by norm_num) hM\n68\t  rw [← add_div, ← add_div, ← hev, div_self hs]\n69\t\n70\t/-! ## A concrete non-vacuous witness: the 2x2 single-edge carrier -/\n71\t\n72\t/-- Single Clifford generator `sigma_x`. -/\n73\tdef gammaW : Fin 1 → Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ := fun _ => !![0, 1; 1, 0]\n74\t\n75\t/-- Constant unit transport `nabla = 1`. -/\n76\tdef nablaW : Fin 1 → Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ := fun _ => 1\n77\t\n78\t/-- Chirality grading `sigma_z`. -/\n79\tdef GammaW : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ := !![1, 0; 0, -1]\n80\t\n81\t/-- Unit turn field `phi = 1`. -/\n82\tdef phiW : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ := 1\n83\t\n84\t/-- Edge metric `g = 2` (from `{sigma_x, sigma_x} = 2`). -/\n85\tdef gW : Fin 1 → Fin 1 → ℂ := fun _ _ => 2\n86\t\n87\t/-- The witness carrier `D = sigma_x + sigma_z` and its blocks satisfy the\n88\tWeitzenboeck identity `4 D² = Q_A + Q_C + 4 Q_T` (all hypotheses of\n89\t`carrier_square_assembly` discharged for the single-edge `2x2` model). -/\n90\ttheorem witness_assembly :\n91\t    (4 : ℂ) • (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW) ^ 2\n92\t      = (∑ e, ∑ f, gW e f • (nablaW e * nablaW f + nablaW f * nablaW e))\n93\t        + (∑ e, ∑ f, (gammaW e * gammaW f - gammaW f * gammaW e)\n94\t            * (nablaW e * nablaW f - nablaW f * nablaW e))\n95\t        + (4 : ℂ) • phiW ^ 2 := by\n96\t  apply carrier_square_assembly (R := ℂ) gammaW nablaW GammaW phiW gW\n97\t  · intro e f\n98\t    fin_cases e; fin_cases f\n99\t    ext i j; fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n100\t      norm_num [gammaW, gW, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two,\n101\t        Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one, Matrix.one_apply, Matrix.smul_apply]\n102\t  · intro e f; fin_cases e; fin_cases f; simp [gammaW, nablaW]\n103\t  · ext i j; fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n104\t      norm_num [GammaW, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two, Matrix.one_apply]\n105\t  · intro e; fin_cases e\n106\t    ext i j; fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n107\t      norm_num [GammaW, gammaW, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two,\n108\t        Matrix.neg_apply]\n109\t  · intro e; fin_cases e; simp [GammaW, nablaW]\n110\t  · intro e; fin_cases e; simp [phiW, gammaW]\n111\t  · simp [GammaW, phiW]\n112\t  · intro e; fin_cases e; simp [nablaW, phiW]\n113\t\n114\t/-- The witness closure share is exactly `0` (single edge -> no closure). -/\n115\ttheorem witness_QC_zero :\n116\t    (∑ e, ∑ f, (gammaW e * gammaW f - gammaW f * gammaW e)\n117\t        * (nablaW e * nablaW f - nablaW f * nablaW e))\n118\t      = (0 : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ) := by\n119\t  simp\n120\t\n121\t/-- **The witness mass budget.** With `ev = trace`, the aperture/closure/\n122\tturn shares of the single-edge `2x2` carrier are `1/2, 0, 1/2` and sum to\n123\tone - a non-vacuous instance of `signed_budget_sum_one`. -/\n124\ttheorem witness_budget_sum_one :\n125\t    (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n126\t          (∑ e, ∑ f, gW e f • (nablaW e * nablaW f + nablaW f * nablaW e))\n127\t        / (4 * (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n128\t            ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n129\t              * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)))\n130\t      + (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n131\t          (∑ e, ∑ f, (gammaW e * gammaW f - gammaW f * gammaW e)\n132\t            * (nablaW e * nablaW f - nablaW f * nablaW e))\n133\t        / (4 * (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n134\t            ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n135\t              * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)))\n136\t      + (4 * (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ) (phiW ^ 2))\n137\t        / (4 * (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n138\t            ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n139\t              * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW))) = 1 := by\n140\t  have hDD : ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n141\t      * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW))\n142\t      = (2 : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ) := by\n143\t    ext i j; fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n144\t      norm_num [solderedNC, gammaW, nablaW, GammaW, phiW,\n145\t        Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two, Matrix.one_apply,\n146\t        Matrix.smul_apply]\n147\t  have hM : (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n148\t      ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n149\t        * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)) ≠ 0 := by\n150\t    rw [hDD]; No positivity is claimed: the shares are signed (Amendment A3);\n`b_C` is the CHROMOMAGNETIC (hyperfine) share, NOT a gluon-energy share\n(Amendment B wording rail).\n\n## Claim boundary\n\n`b_A + b_C + b_T = 1` is proved (abstract + witness). No continuum,\nno hadron-mass claim.\n\n## Provenance\n\nS6 shape from the QCD roadmap (Amendment A3, SevenChallenges finding 7) -\n[comp]; built on the guard-pinned `carrier_square_assembly`\n(`CarrierSquareAssembly.lean`) - [orig].\n-/\n\nimport PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.CarrierSquareAssembly\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.MassBudget\n\nopen Matrix\n\n/-! ## The abstract signed budget -/\n\n/-- **Signed mass-budget decomposition of unity.** From the Weitzenboeck\nidentity `4 . D² = Q_A + Q_C + 4 . Q_T` and a linear expectation `ev` with\n`ev (D * D) != 0`, the aperture/closure/turn shares of `M² = 4 ev(D²)` sum\nto one. Shares are signed - no positivity assumed. -/\ntheorem signed_budget_sum_one {B : Type*} [Ring B] [Algebra ℂ B]\n    (ev : B →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ) (D QA QC QT : B)\n    (hid : (4 : ℂ) • (D * D) = QA + QC + (4 : ℂ) • QT)\n    (hM : ev (D * D) ≠ 0) :\n    ev QA / (4 * ev (D * D)) + ev QC / (4 * ev (D * D))\n        + (4 * ev QT) / (4 * ev (D * D)) = 1 := by\n  have hev : (4 : ℂ) * ev (D * D) = ev QA + ev QC + 4 * ev QT := by\n    have h := congrArg ev hid\n    rw [map_smul, map_add, map_add, map_smul, smul_eq_mul, smul_eq_mul] at h\n    exact h\n  have hs : (4 : ℂ) * ev (D * D) ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero (by norm_num) hM\n  rw [← add_div, ← add_div, ← hev, div_self hs]\n\n/-! ## A concrete non-vacuous witness: the 2x2 single-edge carrier -/\n\n/-- Single Clifford generator `sigma_x`. -/\ndef gammaW : Fin 1 → Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ := fun _ => !![0, 1; 1, 0]\n\n/-- Constant unit transport `nabla = 1`. -/\ndef nablaW : Fin 1 → Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ := fun _ => 1\n\n/-- Chirality grading `sigma_z`. -/\ndef GammaW : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ := !![1, 0; 0, -1]\n\n/-- Unit turn field `phi = 1`. -/\ndef phiW : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ := 1\n\n/-- Edge metric `g = 2` (from `{sigma_x, sigma_x} = 2`). -/\ndef gW : Fin 1 → Fin 1 → ℂ := fun _ _ => 2\n\n/-- The witness carrier `D = sigma_x + sigma_z` and its blocks satisfy the\nWeitzenboeck identity `4 D² = Q_A + Q_C + 4 Q_T` (all hypotheses of\n`carrier_square_assembly` discharged for the single-edge `2x2` model). -/\ntheorem witness_assembly :\n    (4 : ℂ) • (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW) ^ 2\n      = (∑ e, ∑ f, gW e f • (nablaW e * nablaW f + nablaW f * nablaW e))\n        + (∑ e, ∑ f, (gammaW e * gammaW f - gammaW f * gammaW e)\n            * (nablaW e * nablaW f - nablaW f * nablaW e))\n        + (4 : ℂ) • phiW ^ 2 := by\n  apply carrier_square_assembly (R := ℂ) gammaW nablaW GammaW phiW gW\n  · intro e f\n    fin_cases e; fin_cases f\n    ext i j; fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n      norm_num [gammaW, gW, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two,\n        Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one, Matrix.one_apply, Matrix.smul_apply]\n  · intro e f; fin_cases e; fin_cases f; simp [gammaW, nablaW]\n  · ext i j; fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n      norm_num [GammaW, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two, Matrix.one_apply]\n  · intro e; fin_cases e\n    ext i j; fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n      norm_num [GammaW, gammaW, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two,\n        Matrix.neg_apply]\n  · intro e; fin_cases e; simp [GammaW, nablaW]\n  · intro e; fin_cases e; simp [phiW, gammaW]\n  · simp [GammaW, phiW]\n  · intro e; fin_cases e; simp [nablaW, phiW]\n\n/-- The witness closure share is exactly `0` (single edge -> no closure). -/\ntheorem witness_QC_zero :\n    (∑ e, ∑ f, (gammaW e * gammaW f - gammaW f * gammaW e)\n        * (nablaW e * nablaW f - nablaW f * nablaW e))\n      = (0 : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ) := by\n  simp\n\n/-- **The witness mass budget.** With `ev = trace`, the aperture/closure/\nturn shares of the single-edge `2x2` carrier are `1/2, 0, 1/2` and sum to\none - a non-vacuous instance of `signed_budget_sum_one`. -/\ntheorem witness_budget_sum_one :\n    (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n          (∑ e, ∑ f, gW e f • (nablaW e * nablaW f + nablaW f * nablaW e))\n        / (4 * (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n            ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n              * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)))\n      + (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n          (∑ e, ∑ f, (gammaW e * gammaW f - gammaW f * gammaW e)\n            * (nablaW e * nablaW f - nablaW f * nablaW e))\n        / (4 * (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n            ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n              * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)))\n      + (4 * (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ) (phiW ^ 2))\n        / (4 * (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n            ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n              * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW))) = 1 := by\n  have hDD : ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n      * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW))\n      = (2 : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ) := by\n    ext i j; fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n      norm_num [solderedNC, gammaW, nablaW, GammaW, phiW,\n        Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two, Matrix.one_apply,\n        Matrix.smul_apply]\n  have hM : (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n      ((solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n        * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)) ≠ 0 := by\n    rw [hDD]; norm_num [Matrix.traceLinearMap, Matrix.trace_fin_two,\n      Matrix.one_apply, Matrix.smul_apply]\n  have hsq : (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW) ^ 2\n      = (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n        * (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW) := sq _\n  have := signed_budget_sum_one (Matrix.traceLinearMap (Fin 2) ℂ ℂ)\n    (solderedNC gammaW nablaW + GammaW * phiW)\n    (∑ e, ∑ f, gW e f • (nablaW e * nablaW f + nablaW f * nablaW e))\n    (∑ e, ∑ f, (gammaW e * gammaW f - gammaW f * gammaW e)\n      * (nablaW e * nablaW f - nablaW f * nablaW e))\n    (phiW ^ 2) (by rw [← hsq]; exact witness_assembly) hM\n  exact this\n\nend PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.MassBudget\n","numLines":164,"startLine":1,"totalLines":164}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TibHcZqjENP1mw7RWmmnVt","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\t# Finite Banks-Casher count identity (K5 / roadmap S4a)\n3\t\n4\tDRAFT (kernel-clean; no `s o r r y`). The overnight all-mass run's K5,\n5\tper QCD-roadmap Amendment A4 and SevenChallenges finding 6: a FINITE,\n6\tfixed-background, spectral-rail-safe form of the Banks-Casher relation -\n7\tan exact matrix-trace identity with no spectral measure, no thermodynamic\n8\tlimit, no positivity assumption.\n9\t\n10\t## The chain\n11\t\n12\tFor a skew-Hermitian matrix `A` (`Aᴴ = -A`; the stereographically\n13\ttransformed finite Ginsparg-Wilson operator, eigenvalues `i lambda_j`)\n14\tand a real regulator `m` with `m . 1 +- A` invertible:\n15\t\n16\t* `invOf_add_invOf` (pure algebra, any ring): `⅟P + ⅟Q = ⅟P (P+Q) ⅟Q`.\n17\t* `resolvent_sum` / `resolvent_sum_trace`: with `P = m+A`, `Q = m-A`\n18\t  (so `P+Q = 2m` central), `⅟P + ⅟Q = 2m . ⅟(Q P)`, and its trace.\n19\t* `skew_prod`: `(m-A)(m+A) = m² . 1 + Aᴴ A` (positive definite; the\n20\t  \"denominator\" - so `⅟(Q P) = ⅟(m² + Aᴴ A)`, the smooth count operator).\n21\t* `skew_resolvent_conj`: `Tr ⅟(m-A) = conj (Tr ⅟(m+A))`, so the left side\n22\t  of the trace identity is `Tr ⅟(m+A) + conj Tr ⅟(m+A) = 2 Re Tr ⅟(m+A)`.\n23\t* `banks_casher_count` (headline): therefore\n24\t  `Tr ⅟(m+A) + conj Tr ⅟(m+A) = 2m . Tr ⅟((m-A)(m+A))`,\n25\t  i.e. `2 Re Tr ⅟(m+A) = 2m Tr ⅟(m² + Aᴴ A)` (`skew_prod`), i.e.\n26\t  `m V Sigma_m = N_m` with `Sigma_m = (1/V) Re Tr ⅟(m+A)` and\n27\t  `N_m = Tr(m² ⅟(m² + Aᴴ A)) = sum_j m²/(m²+lambda_j²)`.\n28\t\n29\tWhole proof is invertible-element algebra plus `star (⅟x) = ⅟(star x)`;\n30\tno eigenvalues, no spectral theorem.\n31\t\n32\t## Reading and claim boundary\n33\t\n34\t`Re Tr ⅟(m+A)` is the finite regularized condensate (up to `1/V`);\n35\t`Tr(m² ⅟(m²+Aᴴ A))` is the finite smooth near-zero count (`m²+Aᴴ A` is\n36\tpositive definite; each eigenvalue contributes `m²/(m²+lambda²) in (0,1]`,\n37\t`-> 1` as `lambda -> 0`). Finite, FIXED background - NOT the\n38\tinfinite-volume Banks-Casher theorem, not a density of states, not a\n39\tchiral-limit statement. The GW exceptional-mode (`D = 2`) caveat is\n40\tupstream: `A` is assumed already on the nonexceptional subspace.\n41\t\n42\t## Provenance\n43\t\n44\tBanks-Casher (1980) for the physics relation - [import]; the finite\n45\tresolvent form was proposed in the SevenChallenges memo (2026-07-08,\n46\tfinding 6) and roadmap Amendment A4 - [comp]. Sits over\n47\t`GateYM/BanksCasherShadow.lean` (the GW-circle facts).\n48\t-/\n49\t\n50\timport Mathlib\n51\t\n52\tnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.GateYM.FiniteBanksCasherCount\n53\t\n54\topen Matrix\n55\t\n56\tvariable {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]\n57\t\n58\t/-- **Resolvent sum (pure invertible algebra).** For invertible `P, Q` in a\n59\tring, `⅟P + ⅟Q = ⅟P * (P + Q) * ⅟Q`. No commutativity needed. -/\n60\ttheorem invOf_add_invOf {R : Type*} [Ring R] (P Q : R)\n61\t    [Invertible P] [Invertible Q] :\n62\t    ⅟P + ⅟Q = ⅟P * (P + Q) * ⅟Q := by\n63\t  rw [mul_add, add_mul, invOf_mul_self, one_mul, mul_assoc (⅟P) Q (⅟Q),\n64\t    mul_invOf_self, mul_one, add_comm]\n65\t\n66\t/-- **Central resolvent collapse.** If `P + Q = c` with `c` central, then\n67\t`⅟P + ⅟Q = c * ⅟(Q * P)`. -/\n68\ttheorem invOf_add_eq_central_mul {R : Type*} [Ring R] (P Q c : R)\n69\t    [Invertible P] [Invertible Q] [Invertible (Q * P)]\n70\t    (hc : P + Q = c) (hcentral : ∀ x : R, c * x = x * c) :\n71\t    ⅟P + ⅟Q = c * ⅟(Q * P) := by\n72\t  rw [invOf_add_invOf P Q, hc]\n73\t  calc ⅟P * c * ⅟Q = c * ⅟P * ⅟Q := by rw [(hcentral (⅟P)).symm]\n74\t    _ = c * (⅟P * ⅟Q) := by rw [mul_assoc]\n75\t    _ = c * ⅟(Q * P) := by rw [← invOf_mul]\n76\t\n77\t/-- **The resolvent identity.** With `P = m + A`, `Q = m - A`\n78\t(`P + Q = 2m` central), the resolvent sum collapses to `2m . ⅟(Q P)`. -/\n79\ttheorem resolvent_sum (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℂ)\n80\t    [Invertible (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)]\n81\t    [Invertible (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)]\n82\t    [Invertible ((m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n83\t        * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))] :\n84\t    ⅟(m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A) + ⅟(m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n85\t      = (2 * m) • ⅟((m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n86\t          * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)) := by\n87\t  have hc : (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A) + (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n88\t      = (2 * m) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) := by\n89\t    rw [show (2 * m) = m + m by ring, add_smul]; abel\n90\t  have hcentral : ∀ X : Matrix n n ℂ,\n91\t      ((2 * m) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ)) * X = X * ((2 * m) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ)) := by\n92\t    intro X; rw [smul_mul_assoc, one_mul, mul_smul_comm, mul_one]\n93\t  rw [invOf_add_eq_central_mul _ _ _ hc hcentral, smul_mul_assoc, one_mul]\n94\t\n95\t/-- **Trace form of the resolvent identity.** -/\n96\ttheorem resolvent_sum_trace (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℂ)\n97\t    [Invertible (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)]\n98\t    [Invertible (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)]\n99\t    [Invertible ((m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n100\t        * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))] :\n101\t    (⅟(m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)).trace\n102\t        + (⅟(m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)).trace\n103\t      = (2 * m) • (⅟((m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n104\t          * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))).trace := by\n105\t  rw [← Matrix.trace_add, resolvent_sum, Matrix.trace_smul]\n106\t\n107\t/-- **The resolvent denominator is `m² + Aᴴ A`** (positive definite for\n108\treal `m`): `(m - A)(m + A) = m² . 1 + Aᴴ A` when `Aᴴ = -A`. So the count\n109\toperator `⅟(Q P)` is `⅟(m² + Aᴴ A)`. -/\n110\ttheorem skew_prod (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℂ) (hskew : Aᴴ = -A) :\n111\t    (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A) * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)\n112\t      = (m ^ 2) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + Aᴴ * A := by\n113\t  have hexpand : (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A) * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)\n114\t      = (m * m) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A * A := by\n115\t    rw [sub_mul, mul_add, mul_add, smul_mul_smul_comm, mul_one,\n116\t      smul_mul_assoc, mul_smul_comm, mul_one, one_mul]\n117\t    abel\n118\t  rw [hexpand, hskew, neg_mul, ← sub_eq_add_neg, sq]\n119\t\n120\t/-- For real `m`, the `m - A` resolvent trace is the conjugate of the\n121\t`m + A` resolvent trace (`Aᴴ = -A` makes `m - A = (m + A)ᴴ`), so the left\n122\tside of the trace identity is `2 Re Tr ⅟(m + A)`. -/\n123\ttheorem skew_resolvent_conj (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℝ) (hskew : Aᴴ = -A)\n124\t    [Invertible ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)]\n125\t    [Invertible ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)] :\n126\t    (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)).trace\n127\t      = (starRingEnd ℂ) (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)).trace := by\n128\t  have hconj : ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)ᴴ\n129\t      = (m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A := by\n130\t    rw [conjTranspose_add, conjTranspose_smul, conjTranspose_one, hskew,\n131\t      Complex.star_def, Complex.conj_ofReal, sub_eq_add_neg]\n132\t  -- (⅟(m+A))ᴴ is a left inverse of (m-A) = (m+A)ᴴ, hence equals ⅟(m-A)\n133\t  have hleft : (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))ᴴ\n134\t      * ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A) = 1 := by\n135\t    rw [← hconj, ← conjTranspose_mul, mul_invOf_self, conjTranspose_one]\n136\t  have hinv : ⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n137\t      = (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))ᴴ := invOf_eq_left_inv hleft\n138\t  rw [hinv, Matrix.trace_conjTranspose, starRingEnd_apply]\n139\t\n140\t/-- **Finite Banks-Casher count identity (headline).** For skew-Hermitian\n141\t`A` and real `m` with the resolvents invertible,\n142\t\n143\t`Tr ⅟(m + A) + conj Tr ⅟(m + A) = 2m . Tr ⅟((m - A)(m + A))`,\n144\t\n145\twhose left side is `2 Re Tr ⅟(m + A)` (the regularized condensate `Sigma_m`\n146\tup to `1/V`) and whose denominator is `m² + Aᴴ A` (`skew_prod`), giving the\n147\tsmooth near-zero count `N_m`. A finite trace identity - `m V Sigma_m = N_m` -\n148\tspectral-rail safe. -/\n149\ttheorem banks_casher_count (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℝ) (hskew : Aᴴ = -A)\n150\t    [Invertible ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)]\n151\t    [Invertible ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)]\n152\t    [Invertible (((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n153\t        * ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))] :\n154\t    (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)).trace\n155\t        + (starRingEnd ℂ) (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)).trace\n156\t      = (2 * (m : ℂ)) • (⅟(((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n157\t          * ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))).trace := by\n158\t  rw [← skew_resolvent_conj A m hskew]\n159\t  exact resolvent_sum_trace A (m : ℂ)\n160\t\n161\tend PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.GateYM.FiniteBanksCasherCount\n162\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cc03f524-0734-485b-befc-8e302c39824a","uuid":"07f79860-bd06-4270-bcaf-f68d0f5c509e","timestamp":"2026-07-08T06:08:24.068Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\Projects\\StandardModel\\PhysicsSM\\Draft\\NullEdge\\GateYM\\FiniteBanksCasherCount.lean","content":"/-\n# Finite Banks-Casher count identity (K5 / roadmap S4a)\n\nDRAFT (kernel-clean; no `s o r r y`). The overnight all-mass run's K5,\nper QCD-roadmap Amendment A4 and SevenChallenges finding 6: a FINITE,\nfixed-background, spectral-rail-safe form of the Banks-Casher relation -\nan exact matrix-trace identity with no spectral measure, no thermodynamic\nlimit, no positivity assumption.\n\n## The chain\n\nFor a skew-Hermitian matrix `A` (`Aᴴ = -A`; the stereographically\ntransformed finite Ginsparg-Wilson operator, eigenvalues `i lambda_j`)\nand a real regulator `m` with `m . 1 +- A` invertible:\n\n* `invOf_add_invOf` (pure algebra, any ring): `⅟P + ⅟Q = ⅟P (P+Q) ⅟Q`.\n* `resolvent_sum` / `resolvent_sum_trace`: with `P = m+A`, `Q = m-A`\n  (so `P+Q = 2m` central), `⅟P + ⅟Q = 2m . ⅟(Q P)`, and its trace.\n* `skew_prod`: `(m-A)(m+A) = m² . 1 + Aᴴ A` (positive definite; the\n  \"denominator\" - so `⅟(Q P) = ⅟(m² + Aᴴ A)`, the smooth count operator).\n* `skew_resolvent_conj`: `Tr ⅟(m-A) = conj (Tr ⅟(m+A))`, so the left side\n  of the trace identity is `Tr ⅟(m+A) + conj Tr ⅟(m+A) = 2 Re Tr ⅟(m+A)`.\n* `banks_casher_count` (headline): therefore\n  `Tr ⅟(m+A) + conj Tr ⅟(m+A) = 2m . Tr ⅟((m-A)(m+A))`,\n  i.e. `2 Re Tr ⅟(m+A) = 2m Tr ⅟(m² + Aᴴ A)` (`skew_prod`), i.e.\n  `m V Sigma_m = N_m` with `Sigma_m = (1/V) Re Tr ⅟(m+A)` and\n  `N_m = Tr(m² ⅟(m² + Aᴴ A)) = sum_j m²/(m²+lambda_j²)`.\n\nWhole proof is invertible-element algebra plus `star (⅟x) = ⅟(star x)`;\nno eigenvalues, no spectral theorem.\n\n## Reading and claim boundary\n\n`Re Tr ⅟(m+A)` is the finite regularized condensate (up to `1/V`);\n`Tr(m² ⅟(m²+Aᴴ A))` is the finite smooth near-zero count (`m²+Aᴴ A` is\npositive definite; each eigenvalue contributes `m²/(m²+lambda²) in (0,1]`,\n`-> 1` as `lambda -> 0`). Finite, FIXED background - NOT the\ninfinite-volume Banks-Casher theorem, not a density of states, not a\nchiral-limit statement. The GW exceptional-mode (`D = 2`) caveat is\nupstream: `A` is assumed already on the nonexceptional subspace.\n\n## Provenance\n\nBanks-Casher (1980) for the physics relation - [import]; the finite\nresolvent form was proposed in the SevenChallenges memo (2026-07-08,\nfinding 6) and roadmap Amendment A4 - [comp]. Sits over\n`GateYM/BanksCasherShadow.lean` (the GW-circle facts).\n-/\n\nimport Mathlib\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.GateYM.FiniteBanksCasherCount\n\nopen Matrix\n\nvariable {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]\n\n/-- **Resolvent sum (pure invertible algebra).** For invertible `P, Q` in a\nring, `⅟P + ⅟Q = ⅟P * (P + Q) * ⅟Q`. No commutativity needed. -/\ntheorem invOf_add_invOf {R : Type*} [Ring R] (P Q : R)\n    [Invertible P] [Invertible Q] :\n    ⅟P + ⅟Q = ⅟P * (P + Q) * ⅟Q := by\n  rw [mul_add, add_mul, invOf_mul_self, one_mul, mul_assoc (⅟P) Q (⅟Q),\n    mul_invOf_self, mul_one, add_comm]\n\n/-- **Central resolvent collapse.** If `P + Q = c` with `c` central, then\n`⅟P + ⅟Q = c * ⅟(Q * P)`. -/\ntheorem invOf_add_eq_central_mul {R : Type*} [Ring R] (P Q c : R)\n    [Invertible P] [Invertible Q] [Invertible (Q * P)]\n    (hc : P + Q = c) (hcentral : ∀ x : R, c * x = x * c) :\n    ⅟P + ⅟Q = c * ⅟(Q * P) := by\n  rw [invOf_add_invOf P Q, hc]\n  calc ⅟P * c * ⅟Q = c * ⅟P * ⅟Q := by rw [(hcentral (⅟P)).symm]\n    _ = c * (⅟P * ⅟Q) := by rw [mul_assoc]\n    _ = c * ⅟(Q * P) := by rw [← invOf_mul]\n\n/-- **The resolvent identity.** With `P = m + A`, `Q = m - A`\n(`P + Q = 2m` central), the resolvent sum collapses to `2m . ⅟(Q P)`. -/\ntheorem resolvent_sum (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℂ)\n    [Invertible (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)]\n    [Invertible (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)]\n    [Invertible ((m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n        * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))] :\n    ⅟(m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A) + ⅟(m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n      = (2 * m) • ⅟((m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n          * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)) := by\n  have hc : (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A) + (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n      = (2 * m) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) := by\n    rw [show (2 * m) = m + m by ring, add_smul]; abel\n  have hcentral : ∀ X : Matrix n n ℂ,\n      ((2 * m) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ)) * X = X * ((2 * m) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ)) := by\n    intro X; rw [smul_mul_assoc, one_mul, mul_smul_comm, mul_one]\n  rw [invOf_add_eq_central_mul _ _ _ hc hcentral, smul_mul_assoc, one_mul]\n\n/-- **Trace form of the resolvent identity.** -/\ntheorem resolvent_sum_trace (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℂ)\n    [Invertible (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)]\n    [Invertible (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)]\n    [Invertible ((m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n        * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))] :\n    (⅟(m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)).trace\n        + (⅟(m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)).trace\n      = (2 * m) • (⅟((m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n          * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))).trace := by\n  rw [← Matrix.trace_add, resolvent_sum, Matrix.trace_smul]\n\n/-- **The resolvent denominator is `m² + Aᴴ A`** (positive definite for\nreal `m`): `(m - A)(m + A) = m² . 1 + Aᴴ A` when `Aᴴ = -A`. So the count\noperator `⅟(Q P)` is `⅟(m² + Aᴴ A)`. -/\ntheorem skew_prod (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℂ) (hskew : Aᴴ = -A) :\n    (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A) * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)\n      = (m ^ 2) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + Aᴴ * A := by\n  have hexpand : (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A) * (m • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)\n      = (m * m) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A * A := by\n    rw [sub_mul, mul_add, mul_add, smul_mul_smul_comm, mul_one,\n      smul_mul_assoc, mul_smul_comm, mul_one, one_mul]\n    abel\n  rw [hexpand, hskew, neg_mul, ← sub_eq_add_neg, sq]\n\n/-- For real `m`, the `m - A` resolvent trace is the conjugate of the\n`m + A` resolvent trace (`Aᴴ = -A` makes `m - A = (m + A)ᴴ`), so the left\nside of the trace identity is `2 Re Tr ⅟(m + A)`. -/\ntheorem skew_resolvent_conj (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℝ) (hskew : Aᴴ = -A)\n    [Invertible ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)]\n    [Invertible ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)] :\n    (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)).trace\n      = (starRingEnd ℂ) (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)).trace := by\n  have hconj : ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)ᴴ\n      = (m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A := by\n    rw [conjTranspose_add, conjTranspose_smul, conjTranspose_one, hskew,\n      Complex.star_def, Complex.conj_ofReal, sub_eq_add_neg]\n  -- (⅟(m+A))ᴴ is a left inverse of (m-A) = (m+A)ᴴ, hence equals ⅟(m-A)\n  have hleft : (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))ᴴ\n      * ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A) = 1 := by\n    rw [← hconj, ← conjTranspose_mul, mul_invOf_self, conjTranspose_one]\n  have hinv : ⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n      = (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))ᴴ := invOf_eq_left_inv hleft\n  rw [hinv, Matrix.trace_conjTranspose, starRingEnd_apply]\n\n/-- **Finite Banks-Casher count identity (headline).** For skew-Hermitian\n`A` and real `m` with the resolvents invertible,\n\n`Tr ⅟(m + A) + conj Tr ⅟(m + A) = 2m . Tr ⅟((m - A)(m + A))`,\n\nwhose left side is `2 Re Tr ⅟(m + A)` (the regularized condensate `Sigma_m`\nup to `1/V`) and whose denominator is `m² + Aᴴ A` (`skew_prod`), giving the\nsmooth near-zero count `N_m`. A finite trace identity - `m V Sigma_m = N_m` -\nspectral-rail safe. -/\ntheorem banks_casher_count (A : Matrix n n ℂ) (m : ℝ) (hskew : Aᴴ = -A)\n    [Invertible ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)]\n    [Invertible ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)]\n    [Invertible (((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n        * ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))] :\n    (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)).trace\n        + (starRingEnd ℂ) (⅟((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A)).trace\n      = (2 * (m : ℂ)) • (⅟(((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) - A)\n          * ((m : ℂ) • (1 : Matrix n n ℂ) + A))).trace := by\n  rw [← skew_resolvent_conj A m hskew]\n  exact resolvent_sum_trace A (m : ℂ)\n\nend PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.GateYM.FiniteBanksCasherCount\n","numLines":162,"startLine":1,"totalLines":162}}}

A fundamental symmetry is a\nlinear map `J : V -> V` with `J * J = id`; it twists the Hilbert inner product\ninto the Krein form\n\n`kreinForm J x y = inner Complex x (J y)`.\n\nThe Krein adjoint is `A# = J * adjoint(A) * J`.  The main result is the finite\nquartet-completeness identity: for a nilpotent Krein-self-adjoint charge `Q`,\nthe radical of the Krein form restricted to `ker Q` is exactly `range Q`, and\nthe induced form on `ker Q / range Q` is nondegenerate.  Positivity of that\ninduced form is deliberately not claimed here.\n\nProvenance: Aristotle project `38eeb1a6-e465-46b5-bb9a-21e5c490a104`, task\n`5d6b94e8-1b32-4795-aa1a-cbc90fc61de3`, clean-room formalization from\nfinite-dimensional linear algebra, with style context from\n`PhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/Carrier/CarrierIndexProtection.lean`.\n-/\n\nopen scoped InnerProductSpace\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima\n\nvariable {n : ℕ}\n\nlocal notation \"V\" => EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)\n\n/-- The Krein form `B x y = <x, J y>` twisted by the fundamental symmetry `J`. -/\nnoncomputable def kreinForm (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (x y : V) : ℂ :=\n  inner ℂ x (J y)\n\n/-- The Krein adjoint `A# = J * adjoint(A) * J`. -/\nnoncomputable def kreinAdjoint (J A : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) : V →ₗ[ℂ] V :=\n  J ∘ₗ LinearMap.adjoint A ∘ₗ J\n\n/-- The `B`-orthogonal of a submodule `S`: `{x | forall v in S, B v x = 0}`. -/\nnoncomputable def orthoB (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (S : Submodule ℂ V) : Submodule ℂ V :=\n  Submodule.comap J Sᗮ\n\n/-- Membership in the `B`-orthogonal unfolds to the stated pairing condition. -/\ntheorem mem_orthoB (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (S : Submodule ℂ V) (x : V) :\n    x ∈ orthoB J S ↔ ∀ v ∈ S, kreinForm J v x = 0 := by\n  simp only [orthoB, Submodule.mem_comap, Submodule.mem_orthogonal, kreinForm]\n\n/-- The Krein adjunction identity `B (A x) y = B x (A# y)`. -/\ntheorem krein_adjoint_pairing (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (hJinv : J ∘ₗ J = LinearMap.id)\n    (A : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (x y : V) :\n    kreinForm J (A x) y = kreinForm J x (kreinAdjoint J A y) := by\n  have hJJ : ∀ z, J (J z) = z := by\n    exact fun z => LinearMap.congr_fun hJinv z\n  unfold kreinForm kreinAdjoint\n  simp +decide [hJJ, LinearMap.adjoint_inner_right]\n\n/-- `J`, packaged as a linear equivalence via `J * J = id`. -/\nnoncomputable def Jequiv (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (hJinv : J ∘ₗ J = LinearMap.id) :\n    V ≃ₗ[ℂ] V :=\n  LinearEquiv.ofLinear J J hJinv hJinv\n\n/-- The dimension of the `B`-orthogonal of `S` is the Hilbert codimension of `S`. -/\ntheorem finrank_orthoB (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (hJinv : J ∘ₗ J = LinearMap.id)\n    (S : Submodule ℂ V) :\n    Module.finrank ℂ (orthoB J S) = n - Module.finrank ℂ S := by\n  have h_orthoB :\n      Module.finrank ℂ (Submodule.comap J Sᗮ) = Module.finrank ℂ Sᗮ := by\n    have h_orthoB :\n        Submodule.comap J Sᗮ =\n          Submodule.map (Jequiv J hJinv).symm.toLinearMap Sᗮ := by\n      convert Submodule.comap_equiv_eq_map_symm (Jequiv J hJinv) Sᗮ\n    convert LinearEquiv.finrank_map_eq _ _\n    rw [h_orthoB]\n  have := Submodule.finrank_add_finrank_orthogonal S\n  simp_all +decide\n  exact eq_tsub_of_add_eq (by def kreinForm (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (x y : V) : ℂ :=\n40:noncomputable def kreinAdjoint (J A : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) : V →ₗ[ℂ] V :=\n161:theorem finite_kugo_ojima (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (hJinv : J ∘ₗ J = LinearMap.id)\nimport Mathlib\n\n/-!\n# Finite Kugo-Ojima\n\nThis module is a clean-room finite-dimensional formalization of the\nKugo-Ojima quartet mechanism as linear algebra over `Complex`.\n\nWe work on `V := EuclideanSpace Complex (Fin n)`.  A fundamental symmetry is a\nlinear map `J : V -> V` with `J * J = id`; it twists the Hilbert inner product\ninto the Krein form\n\n`kreinForm J x y = inner Complex x (J y)`.\n\nThe Krein adjoint is `A# = J * adjoint(A) * J`.  The main result is the finite\nquartet-completeness identity: for a nilpotent Krein-self-adjoint charge `Q`,\nthe radical of the Krein form restricted to `ker Q` is exactly `range Q`, and\nthe induced form on `ker Q / range Q` is nondegenerate.  Positivity of that\ninduced form is deliberately not claimed here.\n\nProvenance: Aristotle project `38eeb1a6-e465-46b5-bb9a-21e5c490a104`, task\n`5d6b94e8-1b32-4795-aa1a-cbc90fc61de3`, clean-room formalization from\nfinite-dimensional linear algebra, with style context from\n`PhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/Carrier/CarrierIndexProtection.lean`.\n-/\n\nopen scoped InnerProductSpace\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima\n\nvariable {n : ℕ}\n\nlocal notation \"V\" => EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)\n\n/-- The Krein form `B x y = <x, J y>` twisted by the fundamental symmetry `J`. -/\nnoncomputable def kreinForm (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (x y : V) : ℂ :=\n  inner ℂ x (J y)\n\n/-- The Krein adjoint `A# = J * adjoint(A) * J`. -/\nnoncomputable def kreinAdjoint (J A : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) : V →ₗ[ℂ] V :=\n  J ∘ₗ LinearMap.adjoint A ∘ₗ J\n\n/-- The `B`-orthogonal of a submodule `S`: `{x | forall v in S, B v x = 0}`. -/\nnoncomputable def orthoB (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (S : Submodule ℂ V) : Submodule ℂ V :=\n  Submodule.comap J Sᗮ\n\n/-- Membership in the `B`-orthogonal unfolds to the stated pairing condition. -/\ntheorem mem_orthoB (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (S : Submodule ℂ V) (x : V) :\n    x ∈ orthoB J S ↔ ∀ v ∈ S, kreinForm J v x = 0 := by\n  simp only [orthoB, Submodule.mem_comap, Submodule.mem_orthogonal, kreinForm]\n\n/-- The Krein adjunction identity `B (A x) y = B x (A# y)`. -/\ntheorem krein_adjoint_pairing (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (hJinv : J ∘ₗ J = LinearMap.id)\n    (A : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (x y : V) :\n    kreinForm J (A x) y = kreinForm J x (kreinAdjoint J A y) := by\n  have hJJ : ∀ z, J (J z) = z := by\n    exact fun z => LinearMap.congr_fun hJinv z\n  unfold kreinForm kreinAdjoint\n  simp +decide [hJJ, LinearMap.adjoint_inner_right]\n\n/-- `J`, packaged as a linear equivalence via `J * J = id`. -/\nnoncomputable def Jequiv (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (hJinv : J ∘ₗ J = LinearMap.id) :\n    V ≃ₗ[ℂ] V :=\n  LinearEquiv.ofLinear J J hJinv hJinv\n\n/-- The dimension of the `B`-orthogonal of `S` is the Hilbert codimension of `S`. -/\ntheorem finrank_orthoB (J : V →ₗ[ℂ] V) (hJinv : J ∘ₗ J = LinearMap.id)\n    (S : Submodule ℂ V) :\n    Module.finrank ℂ (orthoB J S) = n - Module.finrank ℂ S := by\n  have h_orthoB :\n      Module.finrank ℂ (Submodule.comap J Sᗮ) = Module.finrank ℂ Sᗮ := by\n    have h_orthoB :\n        Submodule.comap J Sᗮ =\n          Submodule.map (Jequiv J hJinv).symm.toLinearMap Sᗮ := by\n      convert Submodule.comap_equiv_eq_map_symm (Jequiv J hJinv) Sᗮ\n    convert LinearEquiv.finrank_map_eq _ _\n    rw [h_orthoB]\n  have := Submodule.finrank_add_finrank_orthogonal S\n  simp_all +decide\n  exact eq_tsub_of_add_eq (by linarith!)","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019pmYZiCP9hSmVqhZyNHRU7","type":"tool_result","content":"PhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/Carrier/CarrierWardDescentWitness.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/BerezinMatthewsSalam.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/FermionicSingleCutRP.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/FiniteNonabelianChar.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/FluxSectorZ2.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/QCNonabelianTwoFace.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/TransferGapDefinition.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/TransferPositivity.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/TwoStateTransferSpectrum.lean\nPhysicsSM/Draft/NullEdge/GateYM/TwoStateTransferWitness.lean\n---\nimport PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KreinPositiveSectorWitness\n\n/-!\n# Ward/descent symmetry witness on the positive-sector model\n\nThis module closes a model-audit gap left open by\n`KreinPositiveSectorWitness.lean`.\n\n`KugoOjima.descent_unitary` proves that a `J`-unitary operator `U` commuting\nwith a nilpotent charge `Q` preserves `ker Q`, preserves `range Q`, and\npreserves the Krein form on representatives.  Until this file, the repository\nhad not exhibited a concrete non-identity operator satisfying those two\nhypotheses on the `(2,1)` positive-sector model.\n\nHere we give such a witness.  For any phase `a` with `star a * a = 1`,\n`Uop a = diag(a, a, 1)` is `Jpos`-unitary and commutes with `Qop`.  Therefore\n`descent_unitary` applies nonvacuously, and every `Uop a` fixes the surviving\nphysical-class representative `e2`.  The special phase `a = Complex.I` gives a\ngenuine non-identity symmetry.\n\nClaim boundary: this is finite linear algebra on `EuclideanSpace C (Fin 3)`.\nIt witnesses the hypotheses of the finite descent lemma.  It does not prove a\nphysical Ward identity, BRST cohomology statement, or state-space positivity\nbeyond the displayed finite model.\n\nProvenance: Aristotle audit lane\n`ne-solo-lane-hstar-gauss-ward-realsplit-model-audit-20260707`, clean-room\nfinite linear algebra built on `KugoOjima.descent_unitary` and the existing\n`KreinPositiveSectorWitness` model.\n-/\n\nopen scoped InnerProductSpace\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima\n\n/-- The diagonal phase matrix `diag(a, a, 1)`. Equal entries in the first two\ndiagonal slots are exactly what makes it commute with the charge `Q = e1 -> e0`. -/\nnoncomputable def Umat (a : ℂ) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ℂ :=\n  !![a, 0, 0; 0, a, 0; 0, 0, 1]\n\n/-- The candidate Ward/descent symmetry operator `Uop a = diag(a, a, 1)`. -/\nnoncomputable def Uop (a : ℂ) : W →ₗ[ℂ] W :=\n  Matrix.toEuclideanLin (Umat a)\n\n/-- `toEuclideanLin` turns matrix multiplication into composition in the `3 x 3`\ncase. -/\nprivate theorem toEuclideanLin_mul3 (M N : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ℂ) :\n    Matrix.toEuclideanLin (M * N)\n      = (a : ℂ) : Uop a ∘ₗ Qop = Qop ∘ₗ Uop a := by\n  rw [Uop, Qop, ← toEuclideanLin_mul3, ← toEuclideanLin_mul3]\n  congr 1\n  ext i j\n  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n    simp [Umat, Qmat, PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KreinPositiveSectorWitness\n\n/-!\n# Ward/descent symmetry witness on the positive-sector model\n\nThis module closes a model-audit gap left open by\n`KreinPositiveSectorWitness.lean`.\n\n`KugoOjima.descent_unitary` proves that a `J`-unitary operator `U` commuting\nwith a nilpotent charge `Q` preserves `ker Q`, preserves `range Q`, and\npreserves the Krein form on representatives.  Until this file, the repository\nhad not exhibited a concrete non-identity operator satisfying those two\nhypotheses on the `(2,1)` positive-sector model.\n\nHere we give such a witness.  For any phase `a` with `star a * a = 1`,\n`Uop a = diag(a, a, 1)` is `Jpos`-unitary and commutes with `Qop`.  Therefore\n`descent_unitary` applies nonvacuously, and every `Uop a` fixes the surviving\nphysical-class representative `e2`.  The special phase `a = Complex.I` gives a\ngenuine non-identity symmetry.\n\nClaim boundary: this is finite linear algebra on `EuclideanSpace C (Fin 3)`.\nIt witnesses the hypotheses of the finite descent lemma.  It does not prove a\nphysical Ward identity, BRST cohomology statement, or state-space positivity\nbeyond the displayed finite model.\n\nProvenance: Aristotle audit lane\n`ne-solo-lane-hstar-gauss-ward-realsplit-model-audit-20260707`, clean-room\nfinite linear algebra built on `KugoOjima.descent_unitary` and the existing\n`KreinPositiveSectorWitness` model.\n-/\n\nopen scoped InnerProductSpace\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.KugoOjima\n\n/-- The diagonal phase matrix `diag(a, a, 1)`. Equal entries in the first two\ndiagonal slots are exactly what makes it commute with the charge `Q = e1 -> e0`. -/\nnoncomputable def Umat (a : ℂ) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ℂ :=\n  !![a, 0, 0; 0, a, 0; 0, 0, 1]\n\n/-- The candidate Ward/descent symmetry operator `Uop a = diag(a, a, 1)`. -/\nnoncomputable def Uop (a : ℂ) : W →ₗ[ℂ] W :=\n  Matrix.toEuclideanLin (Umat a)\n\n/-- `toEuclideanLin` turns matrix multiplication into composition in the `3 x 3`\ncase. -/\nprivate theorem toEuclideanLin_mul3 (M N : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ℂ) :\n    Matrix.toEuclideanLin (M * N)\n      = Matrix.toEuclideanLin M ∘ₗ Matrix.toEuclideanLin N :=\n  Matrix.toLpLin_mul 2 2 2 M N\n\n/-- `Uop a` commutes with the charge `Qop`: the finite Ward commutation\nhypothesis. -/\ntheorem Uop_comm_Qop (a : ℂ) : Uop a ∘ₗ Qop = Qop ∘ₗ Uop a := by\n  rw [Uop, Qop, ← toEuclideanLin_mul3, ← toEuclideanLin_mul3]\n  congr 1\n  ext i j\n  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>\n    simp [Umat, Qmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three]","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016Sif6EK2rEF6nBgzoP2EKh","type":"tool_result","content":"    simp only [star_mul, hphiStar] at h\n    exact h.symm\n  have hGammaNablaStar : ∀ e, Gamma * star (nabla e) = star (nabla e) * Gamma := by\n    intro e\n    have h := congrArg star (hGammaNabla e)\n    simp only [star_mul, hGammaStar] at h\n    exact h.symm\n  -- `star D0 = ∑ e, star (nabla e) * gamma e`.\n  have hstarD0 : star (solderedNC gamma nabla) = ∑ e, star (nabla e) * gamma e := by\n    unfold solderedNC\n    rw [star_sum]\n    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl\n    intro e _\n    rw [star_mul, hgammaStar]\n  -- `Γφ` is Krein self-adjoint.\n  have hstarPhi : star (Gamma * phi) = Gamma * phi := by\n    rw [star_mul, hphiStar, hGammaStar, hPhiComm]\n  -- The aperture + closure blocks from the pair master identity.\n  have hkrein : (4 : R) • (star (solderedNC gamma nabla) * solderedNC gamma nabla)\n      = (∑ e, ∑ f, g e f • (star (nabla e) * nabla f + star (nabla f) * nabla e))\n        + (∑ e, ∑ f, (gamma e * gamma f - gamma f * gamma e)\n            * (star (nabla e) * nabla f - star (nabla f) * nabla e)) := by\n    have hpair := weitzenbock_master_pair gamma (fun e => star (nabla e)) nabla g\n      hcl hcommMStar hcomm\n    simp only [] at hpair\n    rw [hstarD0]\n    rw [show solderedNC gamma nabla = ∑ f, gamma f * nabla f from rfl]\n    exact hpair\n  -- The Krein cross term is the self-adjointness defect `E_#`.\n  have hcross : star (solderedNC gamma nabla) * (Gamma * phi)\n        + (Gamma * phi) * solderedNC gamma nabla\n      = ∑ e, gamma e * Gamma * (phi * (star (nabla e) - nabla e)) := by\n    rw [hstarD0]\n    unfold solderedNC\n    rw [Finset.sum_mul, Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]\n    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl\n    intro e _\n    have e1 : star (nabla e) * gamma e * (Gamma * phi)\n        = gamma e * Gamma * (phi * star (nabla e)) := by\n      have h1 : star (nabla e) * gamma e = gamma e * star (nabla e) := (hcommMStar e e).symm\n      rw [h1]\n      have h2 : gamma e * star (nabla e) * (Gamma * phi)\n          = gamma e * (star (nabla e) * Gamma) * phi := by noncomm_ring\n      rw [h2, ← hGammaNablaStar e]\n      have h3 : gamma e * (Gamma * star (nabla e)) * phi\n          = gamma e * Gamma * (star (nabla e) * phi) := by noncomm_ring\n      rw [h3, hCovStar e]\n    have e2 : Gamma * phi * (gamma e * nabla e)\n        = - (gamma e * Gamma * (phi * nabla e)) := by\n      have h : Gamma * phi * (gamma e * nabla e) = Gamma * (phi * gamma e) * nabla e := by\n        noncomm_ring\n      rw [h, hPhiGamma e]\n      have h2 : Gamma * (gamma e * phi) * nabla e = (Gamma * gamma e) * (phi * nabla e) := by\n        noncomm_ring\n      rw [h2, hGammaAnti e]; noncomm_ring\n    rw [e1, e2]; noncomm_ring\n  -- The potential squares to `phi ^ 2`.\n  have hpotsq : (Gamma * phi) * (Gamma * phi) = phi ^ 2 := by\n    rw [← pow_two]; exact potential_sq Gamma phi hGammaSq hPhiComm\n  -- Assemble the Krein square.\n  rw [star_add, hstarPhi]\n  have expand : (star (solderedNC gamma nabla) + Gamma * phi)\n        * (solderedNC gamma nabla + Gamma * phi)\n      = star (solderedNC gamma nabla) * solderedNC gamma nabla\n        + (star (solderedNC gamma nabla) * (Gamma * phi)\n            + (Gamma * phi) * solderedNC gamma nabla)\n        + (Gamma * phi) * (Gamma * phi) := by noncomm_ring\n  rw [expand, smul_add, smul_add, hkrein, hcross, hpotsq]\n  abel\n\nomit [StarRing R] [StarModule R B] in\n/-- **Self-adjoint corollary: the assembly transports to `D^#D`.**  When every transport\nis Krein-self-adjoint (`star nabla_e = nabla_e`, the edge-reflection gauge class), the\ndefect `E_#` vanishes and the Krein square equals the banked `D^2` assembly:\n`4 • (star D * D) = Q_A + Q_C + 4 Q_T`. -/\ntheorem carrier_krein_square_selfAdjoint (gamma nabla : E → B) (Gamma phi : B) (g : E → E → R)\n    (hcl : ∀ e f, gamma e * gamma f + gamma f * gamma e = algebraMap R B (g e f))\n    (hcomm : ∀ e f, gamma e * nabla f = nabla f * gamma e)\n    (hGammaSq : Gamma * Gamma = 1)\n    (hGammaAnti : ∀ e, Gamma * gamma e = - (gamma e * Gamma))\n    (hGammaNabla : ∀ e, Gamma * nabla e = nabla e * Gamma)\n    (hPhiGamma : ∀ e, phi * gamma e = gamma e * phi)\n    (hPhiComm : Gamma * phi = phi * Gamma)\n    (hCov : ∀ e, nabla e * phi = phi * nabla e)\n    (hgammaStar : ∀ e, star (gamma e) = gamma e)\n    (hGammaStar : star Gamma = Gamma) (hphiStar : star phi = phi)\n    (hnablaStar : ∀ e, star (nabla e) = nabla e) :\n    (4 : R) • (star (solderedNC gamma nabla + Gamma * phi)\n        * (solderedNC gamma nabla + Gamma * phi))\n      = (∑ e, ∑ f, g e f • (nabla e * nabla f + nabla f * nabla e))\n        + (∑ e, ∑ f, (gamma e * gamma f - gamma f * gamma e)\n            * (nabla e * nabla f - nabla f * nabla e))\n        + (4 : R) • phi ^ 2 := by\n  have h := carrier_krein_square gamma nabla Gamma phi g hcl hcomm hGammaSq hGammaAnti\n    hGammaNabla hPhiGamma hPhiComm hCov hgammaStar hGammaStar hphiStar\n  rw [h]\n  simp only [hnablaStar, sub_self, mul_zero, Finset.sum_const_zero, smul_zero, add_zero]\n\nend PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier\n===MASTER===\n  simp +decide [ mul_sub, Finset.sum_sub_distrib ];\n  rw [ ← Finset.sum_comm ] ; exact sub_eq_zero_of_eq ( Finset.sum_congr rfl fun _ _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun _ _ => by rw [ hF ] ) ;\n\n/-\nA double sum of `(antisymmetric F) * (symmetrized G)` vanishes, characteristic-free.\n-/\nlemma sum_antisym_sym_zero (F G : E → E → B) (hF : ∀ e f, F e f = - F f e) :\n    (∑ e, ∑ f, F e f * (G e f + G f e)) = 0 := by\n  simp +decide [ mul_add, Finset.sum_add_distrib ];\n  rw [ add_eq_zero_iff_eq_neg ];\n  rw [ ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib, ← Finset.sum_comm ];\n  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => by rw [ ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib ] ; exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => by rw [ hF ] ; simp +decide ;\n\n/-\n**The discrete Weitzenbock master identity (brick 2b).**  With the Clifford\nanticommutator relation `hcl` (the anticommutator of two soldered generators is the\ncentral scalar image of the Gram/polar datum `g e f`) and the soldering-transport\ncommutation `hcomm` (`gamma e` commutes with every `nabla f`), the square of the\nsoldered operator decomposes as the aperture `Q_A` block (symmetric, built from `g`\nand the `nabla`-anticommutators) plus the closure `Q_C` block (the product of the\nClifford commutators with the transport commutators):\n\n`4 • D0^2 = sum_e sum_f g e f • (nabla e * nabla f + nabla f * nabla e)\n          + sum_e sum_f (gamma e * gamma f - gamma f * gamma e)\n                        * (nabla e * nabla f - nabla f * nabla e).`\n\nCharacteristic-free (the `4 •` avoids all division).\n-/\ntheorem weitzenbock_master (gamma nabla : E → B) (g : E → E → R)\n    (hcl : ∀ e f, gamma e * gamma f + gamma f * gamma e = algebraMap R B (g e f))\n    (hcomm : ∀ e f, gamma e * nabla f = nabla f * gamma e) :\n    (4 : R) • (solderedNC gamma nabla) ^ 2\n      = (∑ e, ∑ f, g e f • (nabla e * nabla f + nabla f * nabla e))\n        + (∑ e, ∑ f, (gamma e * gamma f - gamma f * gamma e)\n            * (nabla e * nabla f - nabla f * nabla e)) := by\n  -- Apply the noncommutative ring identity to each term in the sum.\n  have hsum : ∑ e, ∑ f, (4 : R) • ((gamma e * gamma f) * (nabla e * nabla f)) =\n    (∑ e, ∑ f, (gamma e * gamma f + gamma f * gamma e) * (nabla e * nabla f + nabla f * nabla e)) +\n    (∑ e, ∑ f, (gamma e * gamma f + gamma f * gamma e) * (nabla e * nabla f - nabla f * nabla e)) +\n    (∑ e, ∑ f, (gamma e * gamma f - gamma f * gamma e) * (nabla e * nabla f + nabla f * nabla e)) +\n    (∑ e, ∑ f, (gamma e * gamma f - gamma f * gamma e) * (nabla e * nabla f - nabla f * nabla e)) := by\n      simp +decide only [← Finset.sum_add_distrib] ; congr ; ext e ; congr ; ext f ; ring;\n      simp +decide only [mul_add, add_mul, mul_sub, sub_mul] ; abel_nf;\n      norm_cast;\n  convert hsum using 1;\n  · simp +decide only [solderedNC, pow_two];\n    simp +decide only [Finset.sum_mul _ _ _, Finset.mul_sum];\n    simp +decide only [Finset.smul_sum];\n    refine' Finset.sum_congr rfl fun e _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun f _ => _;\n    grind;\n  · simp +decide only [Algebra.smul_def, hcl];\n    simp +decide [ add_assoc, sum_antisym_sym_zero ];\n    exact sum_sym_antisym_zero _ _ fun e f => by simp +decide [ ← hcl ] ; abel;\n\nend PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cc03f524-0734-485b-befc-8e302c39824a","uuid":"66f5aecb-f1bb-4df4-9fdb-e6d0d7c097a7","timestamp":"2026-07-08T06:14:13.513Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"    simp only [star_mul, hphiStar] at h\n    exact h.symm\n  have hGammaNablaStar : ∀ e, Gamma * star (nabla e) = star (nabla e) * Gamma := by\n    intro e\n    have h := congrArg star (hGammaNabla e)\n    simp only [star_mul, hGammaStar] at h\n    exact h.symm\n  -- `star D0 = ∑ e, star (nabla e) * gamma e`.\n  have hstarD0 : star (solderedNC gamma nabla) = ∑ e, star (nabla e) * gamma e := by\n    unfold solderedNC\n    rw [star_sum]\n    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl\n    intro e _\n    rw [star_mul, hgammaStar]\n  -- `Γφ` is Krein self-adjoint.\n  have hstarPhi : star (Gamma * phi) = Gamma * phi := by\n    rw [star_mul, hphiStar, hGammaStar, hPhiComm]\n  -- The aperture + closure blocks from the pair master identity.\n  have hkrein : (4 : R) • (star (solderedNC gamma nabla) * solderedNC gamma nabla)\n      = (∑ e, ∑ f, g e f • (star (nabla e) * nabla f + star (nabla f) * nabla e))\n        + (∑ e, ∑ f, (gamma e * gamma f - nabla f * nabla e)) := by\n      simp +decide only [← Finset.sum_add_distrib] ; congr ; ext e ; congr ; ext f ; ring;\n      simp +decide only [mul_add, add_mul, mul_sub, sub_mul] ; abel_nf;\n      norm_cast;\n  convert hsum using 1;\n  · simp +decide only [solderedNC, pow_two];\n    simp +decide only [Finset.sum_mul _ _ _, Finset.mul_sum];\n    simp +decide only [Finset.smul_sum];\n    refine' Finset.sum_congr rfl fun e _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun f _ => _;\n    grind;\n  · simp +decide only [Algebra.smul_def, hcl];\n    simp +decide [ add_assoc, sum_antisym_sym_zero ];\n    exact sum_sym_antisym_zero _ _ fun e f => by simp +decide [ ← hcl ] ; abel;\n\nend PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

Positive physical norm can therefore *never* be sourced inside the closure doublet — it must come from the `J`-definite complement of the doublet (transverse/matter directions of the full carrier), exactly as in Gupta-Bleuler the scalar-longitudinal pair contributes zero and the photon's norm lives transversely. This converges precisely with the repo's `(2,1)` vs `(1,2)` witnesses: **the decision quantity is the ambient inertia surplus `p − q` on the doublet-free complement** — the `p > q` nonvacuity MEMO of `KreinPositiveSectorWitness.lean`, now with a mechanism.
